Blocking over i\n// lets the compiler vectorize across i while the summation order over j stays identical\n// to the sequential code, which keeps results bit-for-bit equivalent.\nconstexpr size_t IBLOCK = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank (they are needed by\n// every force evaluation), velocities are distributed: rank r owns [offset, offset+count).\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // global, size n\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // local, size count\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies and applies them to the local\n// velocities. All positions of all bodies must be up to date in state.p*.\nvoid computeForces(State& state, const size_t n, const size_t offset, const size_t count) {\n    const double* __restrict px = state.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = state.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = state.pz.data();\n\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < count; i0 += IBLOCK) {\n        const size_t ib = std::min(IBLOCK, count - i0);\n\n        double xi[IBLOCK], yi[IBLOCK], zi[IBLOCK];\n        double Fx[IBLOCK] = {}, Fy[IBLOCK] = {}, Fz[IBLOCK] = {};\n\n        // The tail block is padded with a copy of its first body so that the kernel below\n        // always runs over the full block width; the padded results are discarded.\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < IBLOCK; ++k) {\n            const size_t idx = offset + i0 + (k < ib ? k : 0);\n            xi[k] = px[idx];\n            yi[k] = py[idx];\n            zi[k] = pz[idx];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < IBLOCK; ++k) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[k];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[k];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < ib; ++k) {\n            state.vx[i0 + k] += DT * Fx[k];\n            state.vy[i0 + k] += DT * Fy[k];\n            state.vz[i0 + k] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& state, const size_t offset, const size_t count) {\n    double* __restrict px = double* __restrict vy = state.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = state.vz.data();\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable Blocking over i\n// lets the compiler vectorize across i while the summation order over j stays identical\n// to the sequential code, which keeps results bit-for-bit equivalent.\nconstexpr size_t IBLOCK = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < ib; ++k) {\n            state.vx[i0 + k] += DT * Fx[k];\n            state.vy[i0 + k] += DT * Fy[k];\n            state.vz[i0 + k] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& state, const size_t offset, const size_t count) {\n    double* __restrict px =      pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cf2TdLcNmq3NPuk87Yn2S","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `nbody/nbody.cpp` and `nbody/CMakeLists.txt` are now an MPI-parallel version; the executable is still `nbody`.\n\n**Parallelization design**\n- **Decomposition:** bodies are block-distributed over ranks (remainder spread over the first ranks). Each rank owns the velocities of its slice; **positions are replicated** on every rank, since every force evaluation needs all of them.\n- **Per step:** each rank computes forces for its own bodies against all `n` positions, updates its local velocities and positions, then one `MPI_Allgatherv` per coordinate array (`MPI_IN_PLACE`, SoA layout — no packing buffers) re-replicates the positions.\n- **Initialization:** the RNG is deterministic, so every rank reproduces the identical initial state locally and keeps its slice — no scatter needed.\n- **Output:** velocities are gathered to rank 0 (`MPI_Gatherv`); positions are already replicated there. Printing, `-r` results and `-v` validation/energy run on rank 0 only, and the exit code is broadcast so all ranks agree.\n- **Kernel:** switched to structure-of-arrays with blocking over 8 local bodies at a time. This lets the compiler vectorize across `i` while each body's summation over `j` keeps the original sequential order — so results are independent of the rank count.\n\n**Results** (2×64-core EPYC 7763, n=20000, s=10): original serial 4734 ms at n=8000 vs 1215 ms for the new kernel (≈3.9× from vectorization alone); n=20000: 7507 ms at 1 rank → 135 ms at 64 ranks → 85 ms at 128 ranks (≈88× over 1 rank).\n\n**Correctness:** output is bit-identical across 1–33 ranks for n = 1, 2, 3, 17, 777, 1024, 4096 (sum/min/max/samples/hash and final energy), including non-divisible and tiny body counts. `-h`, unknown-option and `-v` paths return the same codes as before.\n\nOne caveat worth stating plainly: results are **not** bit-identical to the original binary. The vectorized kernel makes the compiler contract multiply-adds differently than the scalar AoS code did, giving ~1e-13 relative differences (e.g. hash differs, `Final energy 77761608.211442` vs `...212168` at n=1024). Reproducing the original bits exactly would mean keeping the original AoS scalar kernel and giving up the ~4× per-rank speedup; I kept the speedup. If exact hash equality with the pre-existing binary is a hard requirement, say so and I'll revert the kernel layout — the MPI structure stays the same either way."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":247,"cache_read_input_tokens":52371,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":247},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8636cb12-874f-4c83-b343-0a468fb0bbfb","uuid":"e3516db5-2373-48aa-9f09-e87820b027b2","timestamp":"2026-09-14T01:25:24.729Z","request_id":"req_011Cf2TdL1Q79L3vEBHKpVnm"}
